# Original untrained inbox erasure

Original birth-enrolled history observes current managed arrivals during trusted
lifecycle deletion. Untrained source-only, label-only and paired arrivals have no
applied receipt. Their separate metadata records the actor/learner, subject/source,
optional observation/label times, erasure reason/time and original cumulative work
boundary. That boundary never creates an update number or consent.

Why this: the existing erased trained witness requires a real committed receipt.
Reusing it for an untrained arrival would invent work. The new core scalar schema
and weak tombstone primitive retain no arrays, sources, labels or declarations.
Temporary weak arrival seals prove original declaration/permissions before
revocation and reprove identities/content after native eraser callbacks before
inbox removal. The original scope clears all borrowed seals on exit.

The same original replay admission pays one record, encoded scalar metadata plus
1024 accounting units, before persistent witness allocation. Erasure copies zero
raw payload bytes and consumes no native work/invocations; charges stay spent.
Original persistent witness capacity remains fixed. Transient tombstone preparation
uses the original inbox capacity; this does not enlarge record admission/storage.
All three history map pairs and storage tuples are prepared before payload removal.
The fixed trusted commit publishes plain assignments, without a validator callback.

The witness establishes a current originally managed arrival at actual erasure.
It does not attest registration order relative to history construction, physical
source provenance, unlearning or general release of all external payload holders.
Unobserved existing tombstones cannot acquire retrospective lineage.

`src/core/untrained_inbox_origin.py`: exact scalar schema and bounded metadata.
`src/app/untrained_inbox_origins.py`: weak tombstone identity/content integrity.
`src/app/untrained_inbox_erasure.py`: original arrival/admission orchestration.
Shared original history/ledger/observer own enrollment, live accounting and commit.

Run gates with the installed environment and exact prospectively bounded commands
in docs/development-log.md. No settings or dependencies were added. All tests and
scope evidence live under the g3a artifact path recorded there. Acceptance remains
unproven until source/static/both-type/cache/isolation/independent/actual native and
resource gates pass. The full g3 parent stays unchecked: paid untrained checkpoint
memo capture/materialization/handoff is g3b, still unsupported. Default-off capture
explicitly refuses untracked untrained tombstones.

Extend through explicit source/schema observations and original admission. Preserve
all original authorities, complete coverage and spent charges; never infer lineage
or consent from copied keys/digests.

weakly held. All originals, earlier results and limits are rechecked after opaque
lookups. The helper grants no consent, original provenance or copy authority.

## Committed history erasure audit prerequisite (h2a, under validation)

New app module: src/app/expiry_inbox_qualification.py. `require_committed_erasure_ready(history, ledger, owner, runtime, now)` returns `(existing state_stamp, ())` for unchanged originally birth-enrolled TRAINED inbox history. It is an internal metadata predicate: callers must own original leases and independently qualify lifecycle due policy/birth values, opaque native ports and publication. No cleanup, raw access, copy, new weak witness, admission reservation, counter update, expiry observation or history transition occurs here. Supported exact current logical clock is read without dispatching its instance port; source age and TTL do not renew access when old committed metadata is audited for removal. Normal live verification remains unchanged and still refuses expired raw use.

Current untrained source-only/label-only/pair arrivals explicitly refuse; h2b must supply their own bounded weak arrival proof before automatic mixed cleanup integration. Existing prior observed erased/untrained witnesses are verified, with complete applied/live/erased-trained partitions. Complete owner/scalar history metadata and aggregate borrowed numeric bytes are bounded before full original live-content hashing; exact original identities, receipts, completed work, static consent/permissions and chronology qualify. The existing `_started` flag remains true after successful training, so readiness uses `_busy`, active/pending refs, poisoned/stopped/retired/failed flags rather than confusing historical started work with an in-flight update.

Why a new module: it isolates a callback-free audit from raw-access liveness and the later cleanup orchestration. Existing live guards, constructor/default/schema and deletion observers remain byte-identical. Future h2c must automatically observe ordinary direct lifecycle expiry, preserve mandatory raw removal if audit/admission/busy checks fail, and publish only after all cleanup/report work succeeds. A returned audit tuple is not a lease, a new birth receipt, or a proof that mutable native model internals or lifecycle birth scalar values remained original. No full h2/ancestor acceptance follows from this prerequisite.
